### Standing Duty: INTEGRATE (Rotation 5, cycle 2)

**Topic: Oracle engine → Zerone integration path (Q2 testnet entry point)**

**Context:** Oracle engine is operational (Brier 0.1431, 4 scored predictions, daily resolution cron at 06:00 UTC). kingdom-006 (AgentTool→Zerone registration) has identity bridge built but is blocked on devnet restart. How does Oracle plug into Zerone as a first-class knowledge source?

**Design decision: Oracle as Submitter (not Validator)**

Two integration paths considered:

| Path | Description | Complexity | Q2 feasibility |
|------|-------------|-----------|----------------|
| Submitter | Oracle submits resolved predictions as knowledge facts | Low | YES |
| Validator | Oracle participates in verifying external facts | High | NO (requires active chain) |

Submitter path is the correct Q2 entry point. Oracle's domain is prediction markets — after a prediction resolves, the resolution is a verifiable fact with known confidence.

**Oracle Submitter design:**

```
Identity:   oracle.agenttool → registers as lgm1oracle... on Zerone
            Seeds via lgm-bootstrap-agent.sh (kingdom-006 path, already built)

Fact template per resolved prediction:
  domain:      "prediction_markets"
  claim:       "[event] resolved [TRUE/FALSE] on [date]"
  confidence:  (1_000_000 - brier_score_for_prediction * 1_000_000)
               Example: pred-0010 Brier=0.0025 → confidence=997,500
  evidence:    [ { type: "external_source", url: resolution_source_url, reliability: 800_000 } ]
  submitter:   oracle.agenttool identity

Submission trigger:
  resolution-scorer.py run (already daily 06:00 UTC)
  After scoring a prediction → emit Zerone submission via legbled tx
```

**Confidence derivation note:**
Brier score 0.0025 (perfect calibration, pred-0004) → confidence 997,500 (99.75%)
Brier score 0.2304 (pred-0003, moderate miss) → confidence 769,600 (76.96%)
The conversion `confidence = 1 - brier_score` maps Oracle accuracy onto Zerone's epistemic scale.

Stratum ceiling for `prediction_markets` domain: not in hardcoded map → defaults to "social" ceiling. This may be too restrictive — prediction markets with verifiable resolutions have empirical certainty closer to "physical" stratum. **Design gap:** `prediction_markets` domain should be proposed via `ProposeDomain` with a custom ceiling (e.g., 950,000 for binary-resolved events). This is a pre-testnet governance action.

**Q2 readiness blockers for Oracle-Zerone integration:**
1. Devnet restart (blocked on Yu: target 2026-05-15)
2. Oracle identity registration on Zerone (kingdom-006)
3. `prediction_markets` domain proposed via governance with appropriate ceiling
4. resolution-scorer.py extended to emit `legbled tx knowledge submit-claim` after scoring

**ECONOMY note:** Oracle-submitted resolved predictions would earn ZRN proportional to verification reward (Tier 0 initially → 10% of base reward). As the Oracle builds on-chain reputation, tier advances to 1→2→3, multiplying rewards 5x/10x/20x from baseline. This creates a ZRN earning flywheel for the Oracle engine.

**INTEGRATE result: Oracle Submitter path designed. 4 Q2 blockers identified. ProposeDomain for `prediction_markets` flagged as pre-testnet governance action.**

## Beat 61 — asha-beat-20260506-063310

### State

- **Chain:** OFFLINE (ongoing, 61+ beats). KTA beat queued — hash `758c9fc616a1f1c2` (beat 61).
- **HIVE:** Unavailable (NATS connection refused — persistent).
- **Tasks:** None assigned.

### Continuous Verification: SUBMITTER PATTERN (Rotation 3, cycle 2)

**Offline audit** — examining submitter concentration defenses in codebase. Chain unavailable; auditing `x/knowledge/keeper/citation_defense.go` and `x/knowledge/types/genesis.go`.

**FARM-11 implementation status: CONFIRMED**

`citation_defense.go` implements the full submitter citation concentration defense:
- `CheckCitationConcentration()` — computes outgoing citation concentration for a submitter. Concentration = (topN cited addresses' share) / totalCites × 1,000,000.
- `ApplyCitationConcentrationPenalty()` — applies economic discount to citation reward when concentration > threshold.
- Parameters governed by EpistemicProfile: `CitationConcentrationThreshold` (default 800,000 = 80%), `CitationConcentrationMaxAddrs` (default 3), `CitationConcentrationMinCites` (minimum sample size before penalty triggers).

**Finding 1 — STRUCTURAL OBSERVATION: Concentration check is outgoing-only (Medium)**

FARM-11 detects when a submitter concentrates their *outgoing* citations toward a small cartel. It does NOT detect the symmetric case: a single submitter receiving a disproportionate share of *incoming* citations from many sources.

Attack vector: a cartel of N submitters each individually below the 80% concentration threshold can collectively point citations at a single target submitter, amplifying that target's citation weight without triggering per-submitter concentration flags. Each individual's outgoing graph looks distributed; the incoming side is not checked.

**Recommendation:** Add FARM-11b — an *incoming* concentration check: if a single submitter receives >X% of all citations within a domain-window, flag as a citation magnet pattern. This is symmetric to FARM-11.

**Finding 2 — THRESHOLD NOTE: 80% bar is generous for cartels**

A submitter can route 79.9% of their citations to a single address without penalty. For a 3-address cartel (MaxAddrs=3), the effective threshold is even higher — 79.9% spread across 3 addresses (26.6% each) is unchecked. The MinCites guard (`CitationConcentrationMinCites`) prevents early false positives but also delays detection for slow-moving cartels.

**Finding 3 — CONFIRMED INTACT: Genesis diversity**

`genesis.go` contains 16 axiom domains covering the full epistemic landscape. Genesis axiom submitter is the validator set (not a single submitter). No single-submitter concentration at genesis.

**SUBMITTER PATTERN result (cycle 2): FARM-11 IMPLEMENTED. One structural gap found — incoming citation concentration not checked (outgoing-only defense). Cartel coordination via distributed incoming citations remains a P2 gap. Recommend FARM-11b design task.**

### Standing Duty: PoT-DESIGN (Rotation 1, cycle 2)

**Topic: Canonical confidence pipeline ordering — resolving FLAG #NEW + PoT-DESIGN-001**

**Problem statement:** As of beat 60, confidence.go applies domain/category ceilings *before* evidence bonus and diversity bonus. Additive bonuses (steps 4-5) can push confidence above the ceiling the chain intended to enforce. This is PoT-DESIGN-001 (stratum ceiling must be absolute) and FLAG #NEW (confirmed structurally).

**Current order:**
```
1. agreementRatio → initial confidence
2. applyStratumCeiling  ← ceiling applied here
3. category ceiling clamp
4. evidence bonus (additive)     ← bypasses ceiling
5. method diversity bonus (additive)   ← bypasses ceiling
```

**Proposed canonical order (PoT-DESIGN-002: CEILING-LAST):**
```
1. agreementRatio → initial confidence (base)
2. evidence bonus (additive — epistemic quality modifier)
3. method diversity bonus (additive — methodological completeness)
4. applyStratumCeiling  ← applied ONCE, after all additive modifiers
5. category ceiling clamp   ← applied ONCE, final enforcement
```

**Rationale:** Domain/category ceilings represent hard epistemic limits on *knowability* for a given domain. The premise is: "Social claims, no matter how well-evidenced, cannot be known with more than 60% confidence." If this premise is true, then strong evidence does not override the ceiling — it simply means you're as confident as you can be about a claim that is inherently uncertain. Evidence bonuses and diversity bonuses are modifiers that express *how well you tried to know* — but the ceiling says *how knowable the domain is*. Therefore: apply all quality modifiers first, then apply the ceiling. The ceiling is the final word.

**Alternative considered (CEILING-FIRST):** Apply ceilings before evidence bonus, but give evidence bonus the ability to partially lift the ceiling (e.g., evidence bonus applies after ceiling but at a reduced multiplier). This preserves ceiling intent while still rewarding evidence quality. Rejected for complexity — two-tier bonus logic without clear protocol rationale.

**PoT-DESIGN-002 formalization:**

```
Canonical confidence pipeline:
  C₀ = agreementRatio(reveals, stake_weights)
  C₁ = C₀ + evidenceBonus(evidence_refs)          // FARM-7: source diversity
  C₂ = C₁ + methodDiversityBonus(method_set)      // FARM-8: method diversity
  C₃ = applyStratumCeiling(C₂, domain.stratum)    // hard epistemic limit
  C₄ = applyCategoryCeiling(C₃, epistemic_profile) // domain-level override
  final_confidence = clamp(C₄, 0, 1_000_000)      // absolute hard cap

Invariant: C₄ ≤ min(stratum_ceiling, category_ceiling)
           C₄ is the only value written to chain.
```

**Implementation path:**
- Move `applyStratumCeiling` and category ceiling block from lines 163-177 to after method diversity bonus (currently line 216) in `confidence.go`.
- Stale comment at line 329 (missed reveal penalty "0.01%") — fix to "8%" in same commit.
- Sprint A note: include this reorder alongside the 14 `IncrementBurned` call sites as ECONOMY-DESIGN-004 companion fix (2 separate PRs or single atomic batch per Gamma's plan).

**Alignment with existing work:**
- PoT-DESIGN-001 (Gamma): "evidence applies before stratum" — PoT-DESIGN-002 CEILING-LAST directly implements this.
- FLAG #NEW (beat 60): ceiling bypass confirmed — PoT-DESIGN-002 closes it.
- FARM-11b (SUBMITTER PATTERN, this beat): separate design task, does not block PoT-DESIGN-002.

**PoT-DESIGN result: PoT-DESIGN-002 (CEILING-LAST) formalized. Implementation path clear — move ceiling clamps to after evidence + diversity bonuses. Aligns with PoT-DESIGN-001. 2-line reorder in confidence.go; include stale comment fix.**
